their operational needs, our overseas franchisees may sometimes          人轉變為代理商，反之亦然。
convert from a Retail Partner to a distributor, or vice versa.

BUSINESS OUTLOOK                                                      業務展望
Confronting the dynamic macroeconomic and geopolitical                面對不斷變化的宏觀經濟及地緣政治格局，本集團
landscape, the Group has demonstrated significant resilience and      憑藉多年積累的海外擴張經驗、全球多元化佈局及
operational agility, underpinned by years of overseas expansion       強勁的全球運營能力，展示出其強大的風險韌性和
experience, diversified global footprint and robust global            營運敏捷性。展望2026年下半年，我們將繼續專注
operational capabilities. Looking ahead to the second half of 2026,   於門店網絡升級、IP策略及全球化發展，並繼續致
we will continue to focus on store network upgrades, IP strategy      力於追求高質量的增長。
and global development, and remain committed to delivering
high-quality growth.

investment. We have established a set of internal control measures         措施由我們的高級管理層制定。

Foreign Exchange Risk                                                    匯兌風險
Our financial reporting currency is RMB and changes in foreign           我們的財務報告貨幣為人民幣，匯率變動會顯著影
exchange rates can significantly affect our reported results and         響我們報告的業績及綜合趨勢。此外，我們的經營
consolidated trends. In addition, our results of operations, including   業績（包括利潤率）受到匯率波動的影響。我們的國
margins, are affected by the fluctuation in foreign exchange rates.      際業務主要以美元產生收入。一般而言，人民幣兌
Our international operations generate revenues primarily in U.S.         美元貶值對我們的經營業績產生積極影響，而人民
dollars. Generally, a weakening of RMB against U.S. dollar has a         幣兌美元升值則相反。我們並未使用任何衍生金融
positive effect on our results of operations, while a strengthening of   工具對沖有關風險。若我們需要將美元兌換成人民
RMB against U.S. dollar has the opposite effect. We have not used        幣用於營運，人民幣兌美元升值將對我們從兌換中
any derivative financial instruments to hedge exposure to such risk.     獲得的人民幣金額產生不利影響。相反，若我們決
To the extent that we need to convert U.S. dollars into RMB for our      定將人民幣兌換為美元用於支付普通股或美國存託
operations, appreciation of RMB against U.S. dollar would have an        股股息或其他業務目的，則美元兌人民幣升值將對

Conversely, if we decide to convert RMB into U.S. dollars for the

recommends, but does not require, that the roles of chairman of the    規定）董事會主席與行政總裁的角色應有區分且不應

We deviate from this code provision as we do not have a separate       本公司偏離該守則條文，因為我們並無單獨的主席
chairman and chief executive officer and Mr. Ye currently performs     與行政總裁，葉先生現兼任這兩個職務。葉先生為

the balance of power and authority for the present arrangement         的整體情況後，適時繼續審議及考慮區分董事會主

2020 SHARE INCENTIVE PLAN                                             2020年股份激勵計劃
The 2020 Share Incentive Plan was first adopted by the Board          2020年股份激勵計劃於2020年9月首次獲董事會採
in September 2020 and was further amended in June 2022, the           納，並於2022年6月進一步修訂，修訂於上市後生
amendments of which took effect upon the Listing. Since the           效。自上市起，2020年股份激勵計劃受《上市規則》
Listing, the 2020 Share Incentive Plan was subject to Chapter         第17章規限。本公司倚賴《上市規則》新第17章（自

Dividend Policy                                                           股息政策
On March 31, 2026, the Board has approved the amendment                   於2026年3月31日，董事會已批准修訂本公司所採
of the dividend policy adopted by the Company to amend the                納的股息政策，以修訂股息政策中有關經調整淨利
definition of adjusted net profit under the dividend policy, pursuant     潤的定義，據此，本公司可每年宣派及派付金額相
to which the Company could declare and distribute dividends               當於經調整淨利潤約50%的股息（此為非《國際財務
in an amount equal to approximately 50% of the adjusted net               報告準則》指標，定義為期內利潤剔除管理層釐定的
profit (a non-IFRS measure defined as profit for the period after         若干項目（如以股份為基礎的付款、非現金支出、非
excluding certain items as determined by the management such              經營項目及非經常性項目）後的數額），其確切計算
as share-based payments, non-cash charges, non-operating items            方法及金額將由董事會不時根據本公司在宣派及派
and non-recurring items) each year, with the exact calculation and        付股息前的財務表現及現金狀況而釐定及公佈。詳
amount to be determined and announced by the Board from time              情請參閱上文「非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標」一
to time based on the Company’s financial performance and cash             節。
position prior to the declaration and distribution. For details, please
see section headed “Non-IFRS Financial Measures” above.

spread (the “Call Spread”), which was separate from, but is part
and parcel of the Equity Linked Securities.

The Equity Linked Securities were issued to raise funds in order        發行股票掛鈎證券旨在籌集資金，以進一步發展本
to further grow the Company and its operations and to finance           公司及其業務，並為本公司的股份回購計劃提供資
the Company’s share repurchase programs. The Call Spread is             金。一般而言，看漲期權價差預期可減少股份的潛
generally expected to reduce potential dilution to the Shares and       在攤薄及抵銷行使股票掛鈎證券時本公司須支付的
offset cash payments that the Company will be required to make          超出股票掛鈎證券本金金額之現金款項。這將使本
in excess of the principal amount of the Equity Linked Securities       公司具有更大的財務靈活性，並於股票掛鈎證券存
upon exercise of the Equity Linked Securities. This would give the      續期間將本公司對市場波動的風險敞口降至預先釐
Company greater financial flexibility and reduce the risk exposure of   定的範圍。
the Company to market fluctuations during the tenor of the Equity
Linked Securities to a pre-determined range.

1. BASIS OF PREPARATION                                               1. 編製基準
  This interim financial report for the six months ended June 30,           截至2026年6月30日止六個月的本中期財務報

  Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the                《國際會計準則》 （「國際會計準則」）第34號「中
  “Hong Kong Stock Exchange”), including compliance with                    期財務報告」而編製。其於2026年8月28日獲
  International Accounting Standard (“IAS”) 34, Interim financial           授權刊發。
  reporting . It was authorized for issue on August 28, 2026.

   The interim financial report has been prepared in accordance              中期財務報告乃根據截至2025年12月31日止
   with the same accounting policies adopted in the consolidated             年度的綜合財務報表中所採納的相同會計政策
   financial statements for the year ended December 31, 2025,                編製，惟預計將於2026年年度財務報表反映
   except for the accounting policy changes that are expected to             的會計政策變動除外。會計政策變動的詳情載
   be reflected in the 2026 annual financial statements. Details of          於附註2。
   any changes in accounting policies are set out in Note 2.

   The preparation of an interim financial report in conformity              編製符合《國際會計準則》第34號的中期財務
   with IAS 34 requires management to make judgements,                       報告要求管理層作出判斷、估計及假設，而該
   estimates and assumptions that affect the application of                  等判斷、估計及假設會影響政策的應用及所呈
   policies and reported amounts of assets and liabilities, income           報資產及負債、年內截至目前為止的收入及
   and expenses on a year to date basis. Actual results may                  開支的金額。實際結果可能與該等估計有所不
   differ from these estimates.                                              同。

   This interim financial report contains condensed consolidated             中期財務報告包括簡明綜合財務報表及部分解
   financial statements and selected explanatory notes. The                  釋附註，解釋附註包括對了解本集團自截至
   notes include an explanation of events and transactions that              2025年12月31日止年度的綜合財務報表以來
   are significant to an understanding of the changes in financial           的財務狀況及表現變動而言屬重要的事件及交

4. REVENUE                                                           4. 收入
  The Group’s revenue is primarily derived from the sale of                 本集團的收入主要來自通過中華人民共和國
  lifestyle and pop toy products through self-operated stores,             （「中國」）和海外的直營店、加盟店、線下代理

  through the Group’s self-operated online stores on third-party            通過線上代理商進行的線上銷售。其他收入來
  e-commerce platforms and through online distributors. Other               源主要包括來自加盟商和代理商的授權費、基

         to exchange their securities for cash with the settlement                  證券兌換成現金。
         amount (the “Cash Settlement Amount”).

         The Cash Settlement Amount will be in USD and equal                        現金結算金額將以美元計值，等於獲行

         higher of the applicable exercise price per share (subject                 鈎證券的條款及條件作出調整）及指定交

         the Equity Linked Securities), and the volume weighted                     的較高者。現金結算股份為用於計算現

         concept used to calculate the Cash Settlement Amount,

23. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENT                                                  23. 公允價值計量
       (i)    Financial assets and liabilities measured at fair               (i)   按公允價值計量的金融資產及負債
              value
              Fair value hierarchy                                                  公允價值層級
              The following table presents the fair value of the                    下表呈列於各報告期末本集團按經常性
              Group’s financial instruments measured at the end of                  基準計量的金融工具之公允價值，並按
              each reporting period presented on a recurring basis,                 照《國際財務報告準則》第13號，公允價
              categorized into the three-level fair value hierarchy as              值計量中的定義分類為三個公允價值層
              defined in IFRS 13, Fair value measurement.                           級。

              The level into which a fair value measurement is classified           公允價值計量所歸類的層級乃經參考以
              is determined with reference to the observability and                 下估值技術所用輸入數據的可觀察性及
              significance of the inputs used in the valuation technique            重要性而釐定：
              as follows:

              •      Level 1: Fair value measured using only Level 1                •   第一層級：僅使用第一層級輸入數
                     inputs, i.e., unadjusted quoted prices in active                   據（即於計量日期的相同資產或負
                     markets for identical assets or liabilities at the                 債於活躍市場的未經調整報價）計
                     measurement date.                                                  量的公允價值。

              •      Level 2: Fair value measured using Level 2 inputs,             •   第二層級：使用第二層級輸入數據
                     i.e., observable inputs which fail to meet Level                   （即未能達致第一層級的可觀察輸
                     1, and not using significant unobservable inputs.                  入數據）且並非使用重大不可觀察
                     Unobservable inputs are inputs for which market                    輸入數據計量的公允價值。不可觀
                     data are not available.                                            察輸入數據為無市場數據的輸入數

“GMV”                                 for GMV generated under MINISO brand, GMV refers to the total value of
                                      all merchandises sold by us and our Retail Partners and distributors to end-
                                      customers; for GMV generated under TOP TOY brand, GMV refers to the total
                                      value of merchandises sold by us, our Retail Partners and distributors operating
                                      TOP TOY stores or online stores to end-customers, as well as the total value
                                      of all merchandises sold by us to distributors or other customers that do
                                      not operate TOP TOY stores. Both were before deducting sales rebates and
                                      including the value-added taxes and sales taxes collected from consumers, as
                                      applicable, regardless of whether the merchandises are returned
「GMV」                            指    就名創優品品牌下產生的GMV而言，GMV指我們、我們的合夥人及代理商向終端
                                      客戶銷售的所有商品的總價值；就TOP TOY品牌下產生的GMV而言，GMV指我
                                      們、我們的合夥人及運營TOP TOY門店或線上店的代理商向終端客戶銷售的商品
                                      的總價值，以及我們向非經營TOP TOY門店的經銷商或其他客戶銷售的所有商品
                                      的總價值。兩者均未扣除銷售回扣，且包括向消費者收取的增值稅和銷售稅（如適
                                      用），無論商品是否退回
